/// A broken-down local date/time.
pub const DateTime = struct

/// The current local wall-clock date/time (real time of day, DST-correct).
pub fn localTime() DateTime

/// The wall clock: milliseconds since the Unix epoch, UTC (`platform.current.epochMillis`).
pub fn epochMillis() f64

/// Minutes to ADD to local time to reach UTC (JS `getTimezoneOffset()`), i.e.
pub fn timezoneOffsetMinutes() f64

/// Axis-aligned bounding rectangle in screen / world coordinates.
pub const Rectangle = extern struct

pub const NPatchInfo = extern struct

/// Fill the CURRENT viewport (the area `f.window` reports) with `color`.
pub fn clearViewport(f: *Frame, color: Color) void

/// True if `point` lies inside the axis-aligned `rec` (raylib CheckCollisionPointRec).
pub fn checkCollisionPointRec(point: Vec2, rec: types.Rectangle) bool

/// Test if a circle overlaps a rectangle.
pub fn checkCollisionCircleRec(center: Vec2, radius: f32, rec: Rectangle) bool

/// Test if two circles overlap.

/// The high-level color currency - defined in zimrmath (the one home for color + its conversions).
pub fn colorFromHSV(hue: f32, saturation: f32, value: f32) Color

/// Enter 2D camera space: subsequent draws are transformed by `cam`.
pub fn beginMode2D(gl: *WgpuGl, cam: Camera2D) void

pub const BlendMode = enum(u32)

/// Switch the active blend mode for subsequent 2D draws (raylib BeginBlendMode).
pub fn beginBlendMode(gl: *WgpuGl, mode: wgpu.BlendMode) void

/// raylib's `BeginShaderMode` / `EndShaderMode`: a user fragment shader over the ORDINARY 2D batch.
pub const Shader2D = struct

/// raylib's `BeginShaderMode`: everything drawn until `endShaderMode` goes through the user's fragment shader - ordinary `rect`/`circle`/`text`/`texture` draws, no...
pub fn beginShaderMode(gl: *WgpuGl, shader: Shader2D) void

/// raylib's `EndShaderMode`.
pub fn endShaderMode(gl: *WgpuGl) void

pub fn endBlendMode(gl: *WgpuGl) void

pub fn endMode2D(gl: *WgpuGl) void

/// Enter 3D camera space.
pub fn beginMode3D(gl: *WgpuGl, cam: Camera3D) void

/// A spherical orbit camera.
pub const OrbitCamera = struct

pub const OrbitOptions = struct

/// Like `beginMode3D` but drives the depth-tested 3D batch with a caller-built view-projection matrix (column-major, used as `M*v`) instead of deriving one from a ...
pub fn beginMode3DMatrix(gl: *WgpuGl, view_proj: Mat) void

/// Append one flat-shaded triangle (world-space) to the current 3D batch.
pub fn drawTriangle3D(gl: *WgpuGl, a: Vec, b: Vec, c: Vec, color: Color) void

/// Leave 3D space: flush the batch into the depth-isolated pass (one draw), then reopen the 2D pass (preserving the 3D render) and restore the 2D ortho.
pub fn endMode3D(gl: *WgpuGl) void

/// Close the current render pass and reopen a fresh one over the same surface (colour LOADED, not cleared).
pub fn reopenOverlayPass(gl: *WgpuGl) void

/// Restore the 2D renderer's pipeline + bind state IN-PLACE after a custom 3D batch (`beginMode3DMatrix`..`endMode3D`) flushed into the current pass, so subsequent...
pub fn restore2DState(gl: *WgpuGl) void

/// Draw a lit solid cube centred at `center`.
pub fn drawCube(gl: *WgpuGl, center: Vec, d: CubeDesc) void

/// Draw a world-space line segment between two 3D points (unlit, flat colour).
pub fn drawLine3D(gl: *WgpuGl, start: Vec, end: Vec, color: Color) void

/// Draw a ground grid in the XZ plane centred at the origin: `slices` cells per side, `spacing` world units each.
pub fn drawGrid(gl: *WgpuGl, slices: i32, spacing: f32) void

/// Draw the 12 wireframe edges of a cube.
pub fn drawCubeWires(gl: *WgpuGl, center: Vec, d: CubeDesc) void

/// Draw a lit solid sphere.
pub fn drawSphere(gl: *WgpuGl, center: Vec, d: SphereDesc) void

/// Draw a wireframe sphere (latitude + longitude circles).
pub fn drawSphereWires(gl: *WgpuGl, center: Vec, d: SphereDesc) void

/// Draw a lit solid Y-axis cylinder.
pub fn drawCylinder(gl: *WgpuGl, center: Vec, d: CylinderDesc) void

/// Draw a wireframe Y-axis cylinder (top + bottom rings + struts).
pub fn drawCylinderWires(gl: *WgpuGl, center: Vec, d: CylinderDesc) void

/// Textured-3D: a cube with a texture on each face, and a camera-facing textured quad (billboard) - both depth-tested in the immediate 3D pass.
pub fn drawCubeTexture(gl: *WgpuGl, tex: WgpuTexture, center: Vec, size: f32, tint: Color) void

/// Draw a camera-facing textured quad (billboard) at `pos`, `w`x`h`.
pub fn drawBillboard(gl: *WgpuGl, tex: WgpuTexture, right: [3]f32, up: [3]f32, pos: Vec, w: f32, h: f32, tint: Color) void

/// Camera-facing textured quad framing a sprite-atlas cell.
pub fn drawBillboardRec(gl: *WgpuGl, tex: WgpuTexture, right: [3]f32, up: [3]f32, pos: Vec, w: f32, h: f32, uv_min: [2]f32, uv_max: [2]f32, anchor: [2]f32, tint: Color) void

pub fn drawTexturedTriangles(gl: *WgpuGl, tex: WgpuTexture, positions: []const [3]f32, uvs: []const [2]f32, opts: TexTrisDesc) void

pub fn drawTexture(gl: *WgpuGl, tex: WgpuTexture, x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, tint: Color) void

/// Generate a box mesh of size widthxheightxlength centred at the origin - 24 vertices (flat per-face normals), 36 indices.

pub fn genMeshOctahedron(gpa: Allocator) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// A dodecahedron - 20 corners, 12 pentagonal faces.
pub fn genMeshDodecahedron(gpa: Allocator) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// An icosphere - a sphere grown by repeatedly splitting an icosahedron's triangles into four and projecting every new vertex onto the sphere.
pub fn genMeshIcosphere(gpa: Allocator, radius: f32, subdivisions: i32) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// A procedural rock: take an icosphere and shove each vertex in/out along its own direction by a fractal-noise amount.
pub fn genMeshRock(gpa: Allocator, radius: f32, subdivisions: i32, seed: i32) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// Combine two meshes into one; b's indices are offset past a's vertices.
pub fn meshMerge(gpa: Allocator, a: types.Mesh, b: types.Mesh) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// Shift every vertex in place.
pub fn meshTranslate(mesh: *types.Mesh, dx: f32, dy: f32, dz: f32) void

/// Scale every vertex in place; normals are rescaled by the inverse and renormalized so they stay correct under non-uniform scale.
pub fn meshScale(mesh: *types.Mesh, sx: f32, sy: f32, sz: f32) void

/// Rotate every vertex (and normal) in place around an arbitrary axis.
pub fn meshRotate(mesh: *types.Mesh, axis_x: f32, axis_y: f32, axis_z: f32, radians: f32) void

/// Flip winding (reverse each triangle) and negate normals - turns a mesh inside-out, e.g.
pub fn meshInvert(mesh: *types.Mesh) void

/// Axis-aligned bounding box as {min_x, min_y, min_z, max_x, max_y, max_z}.
pub fn meshComputeAabb(mesh: types.Mesh) [6]f32

/// Deep copy (verts/normals/texcoords/indices).
pub fn meshClone(gpa: Allocator, src: types.Mesh) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// "Unweld" a mesh: give every triangle its own three vertices (nothing shared) and compute one flat face normal for each.
pub fn meshUnweld(gpa: Allocator, mesh: types.Mesh) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// "Weld" a mesh: merge vertices sitting within `epsilon` of each other into one and rewrite the triangle indices to point at the survivors.
pub fn meshWeld(gpa: Allocator, mesh: types.Mesh, epsilon: f32) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

/// Drop triangles whose area is below `min_area` - the slivers and zero-area junk that can sneak in after a weld or from bad input.
pub fn meshRemoveDegenerate(gpa: Allocator, mesh: types.Mesh, min_area: f32) Allocator.Error!types.Mesh

pub const Analyser = struct

z.audio_device

/// Shows the sound's state where it can be read on the device - for finding glitches: in the browser, a panel at the top of the page with the path to the speaker, ...
pub fn showDiagnostics(state: *AudioDeviceState) void

pub fn triangle(self: *Canvas, a: Vec2, b: Vec2, c: Vec2, opts: draw2d.ShapeOpts) void

pub fn triangleFilled(self: *Canvas, a: Vec2, b: Vec2, c: Vec2, col: Color) void

/// Plot image-series sink.
pub fn texturedQuad(self: *Canvas, dst: Rect, tex_id: u32, uv0: Vec2, uv1: Vec2, tint: Color) void

/// Unified primitive: draw `sprite` into `dst` with tint.
pub fn image(self: *Canvas, dst: Rectangle, sprite: Sprite, opts: draw2d.ImageOpts) void

/// `image` twin taking loose numbers for the destination.
pub fn imageXYWH(self: *Canvas, x: f32, y: f32, w: f32, h: f32, sprite: Sprite, opts: draw2d.ImageOpts) void

pub fn pushClip(self: *Canvas, r: Rect) void

pub fn popClip(self: *Canvas) void

pub fn text(self: *Canvas, pos: Vec2, s: []const u8, opts: draw2d.TextOpts) void

/// `text`, formatting the string first.
pub fn textf(self: *Canvas, pos: Vec2, comptime fmt: []const u8, args: anytype, opts: draw2d.TextOpts) void

/// Box-downsample the supersampled buffer into a fresh logical-size RGBA8 `Image` (caller frees with `unloadImage`).
pub fn resolve(self: *const Canvas) !Image

/// Resolve + encode to PNG bytes (caller frees the returned slice).
pub fn writePngToMemory(self: *const Canvas) ![]u8

/// Resolve, encode, and write `path`.
pub fn savePng(self: *const Canvas, io: Io, path: []const u8) !void

/// Small comptime utilities.
pub const meta = struct

/// A persistent-key handle table.
pub fn SlotMap(comptime Value: type, comptime options: SlotMapOptions) type

pub const PointerLock = struct

/// Pure-data identity record for a component type.
pub const TypeInfo = struct

/// This pointer can be used as a unique ID identifying a component type.
pub const TypeId = *const TypeInfo

/// Returns the type ID for the given type.
pub inline fn typeId(comptime T: type) *const @This()

pub const Any = struct

/// A tightly packed index for each registered component type.
pub const CompFlag = enum(FlagInt)

/// Comptime helpers for "View" types - user-defined structs of component pointers that drive iteration.
pub const viewLib = struct

/// Persistent handle table keyed by `Entity`.
pub const HandleTab = SlotMap(Entity.Location, .{ .Generation = u8 })

/// Map from archetype (a bitset of CompFlags) to its `ChunkList`.
pub const Arches = struct

/// A chunk of entity data where each entity has the same archetype.
pub const Chunk = opaque

/// An unencoded representation of command buffer commands.
pub const Subcmd = union(enum)

/// A persistent handle to one entity in the world.
pub const Entity = packed struct

/// All chunks for one archetype.
pub const ChunkList = struct

/// Pre-allocated cpool of fixed-size chunks.
pub const ChunkPool = struct

/// A queue of pending entity edits.
pub const CmdBuf = struct

/// The world handle.
pub const Registry = struct

/// Phantom-typed reference to one entity slot in `Entities(T)`.
pub fn Handle(comptime T_primary: type) type

/// A pool-anchored ECS world parameterized on a primary component type.
pub fn Entities(comptime T_primary: type) type

/// Configuration parameters for `NodeWithOptions`.
pub const NodeOptions = struct

/// Build a parent/child tree node parameterized on the name field type.
pub fn NodeWithOptions(node_options: NodeOptions) type

/// The default Node specialization with `?[:0]const u8` names.
pub const Node = NodeWithOptions(.{ .Name = ?[:0]const u8 })

/// Marker component that classifies entities by *type at the type level* - answers "what KIND of thing is this entity?" without storing extra data.
pub const Tag = struct

/// Interface marker - a `fn (T: type) type` is a `GenericRef` if the type it returns has a `Ctx` decl, plus `get` / `getConst` methods with the canonical signature...
pub const GenericRef = fn (T: type) type

/// Comptime check: does `F` look like a valid `GenericRef`? Asserts the shape of `get` and `getConst` only.
pub fn checkGenericRef(F: GenericRef) void

/// A typed reference into ECS-managed data.
pub fn Ref(T: type) type

z.music

/// The music a program has loaded, and the streams they play through: the music functions take this table alone, and reach a music's stream through it, so a table ...
pub const MusicTable = struct

/// Loads a music from a file's bytes - Ogg Vorbis or WAV, told apart by their first bytes (`Format.detect`) - to play through the engine's mixer, as raylib's `Load...
pub fn loadFromMemory(state: *MusicTable, device: *const audio_device.AudioDeviceState, waves_state: *waves.AllocTable, gpa: Allocator, file_type: []const u8, bytes: []const u8) !Music

/// Whether the music is loaded and ready to play: at once, a music loading whole when `loadFromMemory` returns.
pub fn isReady(state: *MusicTable, track: Music) bool

/// Whether the Music slot is allocated and not yet unloaded.
pub fn isValid(state: *const MusicTable, track: Music) bool

/// Frees the music: its stream - whose ring waits for its voice to end - its source and its file's bytes.
pub fn unload(state: *MusicTable, track: Music) void

/// Starts the music from its beginning - or from where a `seek` last put it - and starts writing its frames into its stream; a music already playing starts over, i...
pub fn play(state: *MusicTable, track: Music) void

/// Stops the music: its stream stopped, its frames still queued dropped, and its position back at the beginning - the next `play` starts from there.
pub fn stop(state: *MusicTable, track: Music) void

/// Moves the music to `position_seconds`, kept within its length.
pub fn seek(state: *MusicTable, track: Music, position_seconds: f32) void

/// Pauses the music where it is, its frames still queued kept.
pub fn pause(state: *MusicTable, track: Music) void

/// Resumes a paused music.
pub fn resumeMusic(state: *MusicTable, track: Music) void

/// Whether the music is playing: begun, not paused, not stopped, not played out.
pub fn isPlaying(state: *const MusicTable, track: Music) bool

/// Keeps the music playing - called each frame, as raylib's `UpdateMusicStream` is.
pub fn update(state: *MusicTable, track: Music) void

/// How far the music has played, in seconds: the frames written since it began, less those still waiting in its ring, from where it began - round its length again ...
pub fn getTimePlayed(state: *const MusicTable, track: Music) f32

/// The music's length in seconds: its frames over its rate.
pub fn getTimeLength(state: *const MusicTable, track: Music) f32

/// Sets the music's volume, 0 to 1 and kept there, which its stream plays at.
pub fn setVolume(state: *MusicTable, track: Music, volume: f32) void

/// Sets the music's pitch, a rate of play - 1 as recorded - kept from going to 0 or below, which its stream plays at.
pub fn setPitch(state: *MusicTable, track: Music, pitch: f32) void

/// Sets the music's pan, -1 left to 1 right and kept there, which its stream plays at.
pub fn setPan(state: *MusicTable, track: Music, pan: f32) void

/// Sets whether the music starts over at its end (`update`).
pub fn setLooping(state: *MusicTable, track: Music, looping: bool) void

/// Compile-time master switch.
pub const enabled: bool

pub fn unfreeze() void

pub fn isFrozen() bool

/// Arm auto-freeze: the next frame whose duration exceeds `factor` x the smoothed baseline (and is at least `floor_ms`) freezes the profiler automatically.
pub fn armAutoFreeze(factor: f64, floor_ms: f64) void

pub fn disarmAutoFreeze() void

pub fn isAutoArmed() bool

/// True when the current frozen snapshot was triggered by a spike (not a manual freeze).
pub fn autoFroze() bool

/// Feed the latest GPU pass time (ms) from the timestamp-query readback.
pub fn recordGpuMs(ms: f64) void

/// The most recent GPU pass-time sample (ms), or 0 if none.
pub fn gpuMsLast() f64

/// Mean GPU pass time (ms) over the recent sample ring, or 0 if none.
pub fn gpuMsMean() f64

/// Mean CPU frame time (ms) across the rolling window, or 0 if none.
pub fn frameMeanMs() f64

/// Clear all recorded state (used by tests and a manual reset button).
pub fn reset() void

/// Number of valid frames currently retained.
pub fn frameCount() usize

/// The longest frame within the rolling ~2s window, or null if none yet.
pub fn worstFrame() ?Frame

/// Iterator over one frame's zones (handles the shared zone ring's wrap).
pub const ZoneIter = struct

/// Zones belonging to `f`, oldest-first (depth order is begin order).
pub fn frameZones(f: Frame) ZoneIter

/// The interned source location for a zone's `src` id.
pub fn srcOf(id: u32) SourceLoc

/// The i-th most recent retained frame (i = 0 is newest), or null.
pub fn frameAt(i: usize) ?Frame

/// Per-source-location aggregate over the rolling window (the "statistics").
pub const SrcStat = struct

/// Accumulate per-call-site stats across every frame in the ~2s window into `out` (indexed by src id).
pub fn aggregate(out: []SrcStat) usize

/// Fill `out` with one value per frame in the rolling window: the total (inclusive) time spent in zones matching `src` that frame, newest first.
pub fn frameSeriesFor(src: u32, out: []f64) usize

z.profiler_ui

/// Draw the flamegraph (icicle: time on x, depth downward) for one frame at the current cursor, filling the available content width.
pub fn flamegraph(u: Ui, frame: Frame) void

/// A horizontal history of recent frame durations (newest at the right), green under the 60fps budget, red over it, with a budget guide line.
pub fn frameStrip(u: Ui) void

/// Per-zone statistics across the rolling window, ranked by total time - the "what to optimise" report.
pub fn statsTable(u: Ui) void

/// The default profiler view: header + frame strip, then a tab bar with the worst-frame flamegraph and the window statistics.
pub fn panel(u: Ui) void

pub const Data = struct

/// Forward kinematics: world pose of every body, joint anchor, joint axis and site.
pub fn kinematics(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Build the shared frame each kinematic tree's dynamics are expressed in, and put every body's inertia and every DOF's motion axis into it.
pub fn comPos(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Tendon lengths and rates, from the constant coefficient matrix.
pub fn tendonLengths(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Composite Rigid Body: build the joint-space inertia matrix M.
pub fn crb(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Expand the packed lower triangle into a dense `nv x nv` matrix, row-major.
pub fn massMatrixDense(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, out: []f32) void

/// Factor `M + damping_dt * diag(B)`.
pub fn factorMDamped(m: *const Model, d: *Data, damping_dt: f32) void

pub fn factorM(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Solve `M*x = y` in place, using the factorization from `factorM`.
pub fn solveM(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, x: []f32) void

/// A cheap estimate of `M`'s condition number: the spread of the factorization's diagonal.
pub fn conditionEstimate(m: *const Model, d: *const Data) f32

/// Body velocities and the rate of change of each DOF's motion axis, in the shared frame.
pub fn comVel(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Compute `c(q,v)` - Coriolis, centrifugal and gravitational forces - into `Data.bias_force`.
pub fn biasForce(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Multiply by the mass matrix: `out = M*x`.
pub fn mulM(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, x: []const f32, out: []f32) void

/// Inverse dynamics: the joint forces that would produce acceleration `acc`.
pub fn inverseDynamics(m: *const Model, d: *Data, acc: []const f32, out: []f32) void

/// Forward dynamics: solve for the acceleration, given the forces.
pub fn forwardDynamics(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Joint springs and dampers into `Data.passive_force`.
pub fn passive(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Read each actuator's transmission coordinate and its rate.
pub fn transmission(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Compute the actuator forces, and the RATE of change of each activation state.
pub fn actuation(m: *const Model, d: *Data, dt: f32) void

/// Set an actuator's control by name (or index).
pub fn setCtrl(m: *const Model, d: *Data, actuator: anytype, value: f32) void

/// Jacobian of an arbitrary world-space point rigidly attached to `body`.
pub fn jacPoint(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, body: u32, point: Vec, jac_p: ?[]Vec, jac_r: ?[]Vec) void

/// Jacobian of a body's centre of mass - the one MuJoCo's `mj_jacBodyCom` returns, and the natural target when you care about where the mass is rather than where t...
pub fn jacBodyCom(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, body: u32, jac_p: ?[]Vec, jac_r: ?[]Vec) void

/// Jacobian of a site.
pub fn jacSite(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, site: anytype, jac_p: ?[]Vec, jac_r: ?[]Vec) void

/// Map a force and torque applied at a point back into joint forces: `tau += J^T*(f, t)`.
pub fn applyForceAtPoint(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, body: u32, point: Vec, force: Vec, torque: Vec, jac_p: []Vec, jac_r: []Vec, out: []f32) void

/// Propagate the SOLVED acceleration down the tree, giving each body's spatial acceleration.

/// Advance `pos` by `vel` over `dt`, respecting the geometry of each joint type.

/// Renormalize every quaternion in `pos`.
pub fn normalizeQuats(m: *const Model, pos: []f32) void

/// Run everything that depends on position and velocity, ending with the acceleration.
pub fn forward(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// The half of the pipeline that depends only on the STATE - everything through the bias forces, stopping before any force is committed.
pub fn step1(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// The half that depends on forces: actuation, the acceleration solve, and the integration.
pub fn step2(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Advance the simulation by one timestep: `forward`, then integrate.
pub fn step(m: *const Model, d: *Data) void

/// Total mechanical energy, for testing and for a HUD.

pub fn bodyLowestPoints(m: *const Model, d: *const Data, out: []f32) void

/// A collision/inertia shape attached to a body.

/// Lift a free-rooted robot so its lowest point sits `clearance` above the floor - a pose copied from a capture puts feet INTO the floor (Geno's soles rest 4.5 mm ...
pub fn restOnFloor(m: *const Model, d: *Data, clearance: f32) f32

/// `out = (to - from) / h`, in the TANGENT space - the configuration block through the quaternion logarithm, the rest by subtraction.
pub fn stateDiff(m: *const rbt.Model, out: []f32, from: []const f32, to: []const f32, h: f32) void

/// Linearise one step of `m` about the state and control currently in `d`.
pub fn transition(m: *const rbt.Model, d: *rbt.Data, out: *Transition, options: DerivativeOptions) void

/// A quadratic tracking cost with diagonal weights, shared by every planner built on this.
pub const Weights = struct

pub const Limits = struct

pub const Problem = struct

/// Everything the backward pass writes.
pub const Gains = struct

/// What one backward pass predicts its step will buy, split into the terms linear and quadratic in the step size: `dJ(alpha) = alpha*d1 + 1/2*alpha^2*d2`.
pub const Predicted = struct

pub const max_stack_ndx: usize

pub const Scratch = struct

z.sounds

pub const SoundTable = struct

/// A sound of `wave`'s samples, kept as the mixer reads them - at the wave's own rate, which the mixer steps through at its own (no resampling at load), and with o...
pub fn loadFromWave(state: *SoundTable, device: *const audio_device.AudioDeviceState, gpa: Allocator, wave: Wave) !Sound

/// A sound from a file's bytes in memory: WAV, decoded at once.
pub fn loadFromMemory(state: *SoundTable, device: *const audio_device.AudioDeviceState, waves_state: *waves.AllocTable, gpa: Allocator, file_type: []const u8, bytes: []const u8) !Sound

/// A second sound sharing `source`'s samples, with playback parameters of its own - for playing one sound at several places or pitches.
pub fn loadAlias(state: *SoundTable, source: Sound) Sound

/// Whether `sound` has samples to play.
pub fn isReady(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound) bool

pub fn isValid(state: *const SoundTable, sound: Sound) bool

/// Frees `sound`.
pub fn unload(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound) void

/// Starts `sound` playing at its volume, pitch and pan, on a voice of its own - beside any earlier play still sounding.
pub fn play(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound) void

/// Stops the sound's most recent voice.
pub fn stop(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound) void

/// Pauses the sound's most recent voice where it is.
pub fn pause(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound) void

/// Resumes the sound's most recent voice where it paused.
pub fn resumeSound(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound) void

/// Whether the sound's most recent voice plays: started, not stopped, not paused, not finished.
pub fn isPlaying(state: *const SoundTable, sound: Sound) bool

/// The sound's volume, from 0 to 10, for its later plays and its most recent voice.
pub fn setVolume(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound, volume: f32) void

/// The sound's pitch, from 1/16 to 16, for its later plays and its most recent voice.
pub fn setPitch(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound, pitch: f32) void

/// Whether the sound loops: a zimr addition to raylib's sounds, which play once.
pub fn setLooping(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound, is_looping: bool) void

/// The sound's pan, from -1 (left) to 1 (right), for its later plays and its most recent voice.
pub fn setPan(state: *SoundTable, sound: Sound, pan: f32) void

z.streams

pub const StreamTable = struct

/// A new AudioStream of `channels` channels - one or two - at `sample_rate`, played on the engine's mixer, which `device` names.
pub fn load(state: *StreamTable, device: *const audio_device.AudioDeviceState, sample_rate: u32, sample_size: u32, channels: u32) AudioStream

/// Frees the stream: its ring retired - freed once its voice, stopped, has ended (`StreamTable.retireRing`) - and its slot released.
pub fn unload(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) void

/// Whether `stream` names a stream that is loaded: its handle names a slot, and the slot holds a stream - not one `unload` has freed.
pub fn isValid(state: *const StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) bool

/// Whether the stream wants more frames: while half its ring or more is free - so a game that tops it up each frame keeps between half the ring and all of it queue...
pub fn isProcessed(state: *const StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) bool

/// Writes `frames` - interleaved f32, at the stream's rate and channels - into its ring, made at the first frames, and starts its voice if it has none: the stream ...
pub fn update(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream, frames: []const f32) void

/// Pauses the stream: its voice where it is, its ring kept; it takes no frames until it plays again.
pub fn pause(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) void

/// Plays the stream on: a paused one resumed, a stopped or never fed one started with its next frames.
pub fn play(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) void

/// Stops the stream, and its ring with its voice: frames queued before the stop are not heard after it.
pub fn stop(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) void

/// Resumes a paused stream, where it paused.
pub fn resumeStream(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) void

/// Whether the stream plays: its voice started, not paused, and playing still.
pub fn isPlaying(state: *const StreamTable, stream: AudioStream) bool

/// Sets the stream's volume, clamped to [0.0, 10.0]: its voice's at once, and the one its voice starts with.
pub fn setVolume(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream, volume: f32) void

/// Sets the stream's pitch, clamped to [0.0625, 16.0]: its voice's at once, and the one its voice starts with.
pub fn setPitch(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream, pitch: f32) void

/// Sets the stream's pan, clamped to [-1.0, 1.0]: its voice's at once, and the one its voice starts with.
pub fn setPan(state: *StreamTable, stream: AudioStream, pan: f32) void

pub fn loadFromMemory(state: *AllocTable, gpa: Allocator, file_type: []const u8, bytes: []const u8) !Wave

/// Free a Wave's sample data.
pub fn unload(state: *AllocTable, wave: Wave) void

/// Whether the Wave's parameters describe loaded, well-formed audio.
pub fn isValid(wave: Wave) bool

/// Deep-copy a Wave (allocates fresh sample data).
pub fn copy(state: *AllocTable, gpa: Allocator, wave: Wave) !Wave

/// Crop the wave in-place to the frame range `[init_frame, final_frame)`.
pub fn crop(state: *AllocTable, gpa: Allocator, wave: *Wave, init_frame: u32, final_frame: u32) !void

/// Convert the wave to a desired sample rate / bit depth / channel count, in place.
pub fn format(state: *AllocTable, gpa: Allocator, wave: *Wave, sample_rate: u32, sample_size: u32, channels: u32) !void

/// Decode a Wave to a fresh `[]f32` interleaved (per-channel layout matches the wave's channel count).
pub fn loadSamples(gpa: Allocator, wave: Wave) ![]f32

/// Free a buffer returned by `loadSamples`.
pub fn unloadSamples(gpa: Allocator, samples: []f32) void

/// Encode a Wave to RIFF/WAVE bytes.
pub fn exportToMemory(gpa: Allocator, wave: Wave) ![]u8

/// How many channels `mixerSamplesOf` gives for `wave`: 1 for a mono wave, so that a pan places it at constant power, and 2 for any wider one, balanced (`mixer.pan...
pub fn mixerChannelsOf(wave: Wave) u8

/// The wave's samples as the mixer reads them (`mixer.Samples`): interleaved 32-bit floats from -1 to 1, at the wave's own rate, with `mixerChannelsOf(wave)` chann...
pub fn mixerSamplesOf(gpa: Allocator, wave: Wave) ![]f32

/// Apply a filter function in-place, freeing the original sample data once the filter has produced its replacement.
pub fn filter(state: *AllocTable, gpa: Allocator, wave: *Wave, comptime filter_fn: anytype, args: anytype) !void

z.ws

/// A socket's handle; 0 is no socket, and reads closed.
pub const Handle = u32

/// A WebSocket connection's state: connecting until its opening handshake ends, then open, and closed once the connection ends or fails to open - and for a handle ...
pub const State = enum(i32)

/// Opens a socket to `url` and returns at once; it connects in the background - poll `state` until open.
pub fn open(url: []const u8) Handle

pub fn state(handle: Handle) State

/// Sends `bytes` as a text message; dropped before the socket is open.
pub fn send(handle: Handle, bytes: []const u8) void

/// The oldest received message, copied into `out`; null when none waits, or when it is longer than `out` (it is then dropped).
pub fn poll(handle: Handle, out: []u8) ?[]const u8

/// Closes the socket and frees its handle; safe to call more than once.
pub fn close(handle: Handle) void

/// The address of the server the app came from - the page's origin in the browser, the signaling server on this machine natively - written into `out`.
pub fn originUrl(out: []u8) []const u8

pub fn destroyChain(world: *World, body: BodyHandle, chain_id: u32) void

/// Remove a body from simulation without destroying it: tear down its contacts (waking neighbours), drop its broad-phase proxies, and take it out of the active set...
pub fn disableBody(world: *World, body: BodyHandle) void

/// Restore a disabled body: rebuild its proxies (forcing pair re-creation) and, if movable, return it to the active set awake.

/// An oriented plane: points on the surface satisfy dot(normal, p) == offset.
pub const Plane = struct

/// One collision result from collideMover: the plane between the mover and a shape, plus the contact point.
pub const PlaneResult = struct

/// A plane fed to solvePlanes.
pub const CollisionPlane = struct

/// Result of solvePlanes: the resolved mover translation and the iteration count used.
pub const PlaneSolverResult = struct

/// Resolve a mover position satisfying the given planes, starting from `target_delta` (the desired movement from the position the planes were generated at).
pub fn solvePlanes(target_delta: Vec2, planes: []CollisionPlane) PlaneSolverResult

/// Project a velocity onto the planes that pushed the mover, removing the components that drive into them (so the mover slides along surfaces).
pub fn clipVector(vector: Vec2, planes: []const CollisionPlane) Vec2
